CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告書



To cope with the unfavourable macroeconomic environment in 2019,            面對 2019 年不利的宏觀經濟環境，本集團充分利
the Group proactively diversified its product range and enhanced its        用強大的品牌實力及廣泛的經銷商網絡優勢，積
competitive advantage by leveraging its brand strength and its              極擴闊產品系列，提升品牌競爭優勢。為應對不
extensive dealer network. In order to adapt to the ever-changing            斷變化的市場需求，本集團亦致力開拓專業市場
market demand, the Group also devoted itself to developing the              平台業務，發掘新的收入增長點，並積極拓展中
business of specialty sales and marketing platform, seeking new growth      國及海外市場，為未來集團收入及盈利的增長打

WHISTLEBLOWING POLICY                                                      舉報政策

The Group has formulated a “whistleblowing policy” whereby                 本集團制訂「舉報政策」，據此，僱員可發送電子
employees can report any matters to the chairman or the Audit              郵件至指定電郵地址或以電話方式秘密向主席或
Committee secretly by sending electronic mails to the designated           審核委員會舉報任何事項，而無需擔心被指責。
address or by phone without fear of being accused. Such matters may        該等事項包括財務事宜及會計實務方面之行為失
include misconduct, irregular conduct or fraud regarding financial         當、不正當行為或欺詐。任何股東或權益持有人
matters and accounting practices. Any Shareholder or stakeholder can       亦可秘密以書面或口頭方式向審核委員會舉報類

(2016–2020)”, the National Development and Reform Commission                 網改造計劃，預期將釋放大量市場需求。
estimates that investments in water pollution prevention and control
projects in major river basins can total around RMB700 billion if the

governments in China have embarked on plans for restructuring
pipeline networks. This can generate enormous demand.

in relation to the establishment of a trust (the “Trust”). Pursuant to the   據」）。根據該計劃的規則，信託人將以本公司出
rules of the Scheme, Shares will be purchased by the Trustee from the        資的現金自公開市場購入不超過 7.50 億港元的股

a discretionary scheme of the Company and shall be subject to the            該計劃並不構成上市規則第 17 章所界定的購股權

     Hong Kong and the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong                 允價值計量的投資物業、其他金融資產及衍
     Companies Ordinance. They have been prepared under the                     生金融工具除外。本綜合財務報表乃以人民
     historical cost convention, except for investment properties,              幣呈列，除另有指明外，所有價值均調整至
     other financial assets and derivative financial instruments which          最接近的千元數。
     have been measured at fair value. These consolidated financial
     statements are presented in Renminbi and all values are rounded
     to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (Continued)                                        2.1 編製基準（續）

   (the “Facility Agreement”), in relation to dual currency syndicated        資協議（「融資協議」），內容有關金額相當於
   term loan and revolving credit facilities in the equivalent amount         900,000,000 美 元的雙 幣銀 團定期貸款 及循
   of US$900,000,000 (the “Facilities”). The final maturity date under        環信貸融資（「融資」）。融資協議的最後到期
   the Facility Agreement is the date falling 48 months after the first       日為首次動用日期起計 48 個月屆滿當日。於
   utilisation date. On 23 October 2019, an amendment agreement               2019 年 10 月 23 日，有關融資協議的修訂協
   in relation to the Facility Agreement was entered into to increase         議 訂 立， 將 融 資 之 總 承 擔 額 增 至 相 當 於
   the total commitment under the Facilities to the equivalent                1,100,000,000 美元以作一般企業用途（其中
   amount of US$1,100,000,000 for general corporate purpose,                  包括本集團現有財務負債再融資）。本公司
   including the refinancing of the Group’s existing financial                董事相信，本集團可繼續從業務營運產生正
   indebtedness. The directors of the Company believe the Group               向現金流。在此基礎上，本公司董事認為本
   will be able to continue to generate positive cash flows from its          集團有能力承擔將於未來 12 個月到期的全
   operations. On this basis, the directors of the Company consider           部債務。因此，本公司董事按持續經營基準
   that the Group is able to meet in full its financial obligations as        編製綜合財務報表。
   they fall due in the coming 12 months. Accordingly, the
   consolidated financial statements have been prepared by the
   directors of the Company on a going concern basis.

   BASIS OF CONSOLIDATION                                                     綜合基準
   The consolidated financial statements include the financial                綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司截至
   statements of the Company and its subsidiaries for the year                2019 年 12 月 31 日止年度的財務報表。
   ended 31 December 2019.

   A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly or     附屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包
   indirectly controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when             括結構性實體）。當本集團承受或享有參與
   the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its          投資對象業務所得的可變回報，且能透過對
   involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect those          投資對象的權力（即賦予本集團現有能力主
   returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights         導投資對象相關活動的既存權利）影響該等
   that give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant             回報時，即取得控制權。
   activities of the investee).

           Therefore, the definition of a lease under HKFRS 16 has                  港（國際財務報告 詮釋委員會）－詮釋

   INVESTMENTS IN ASSOCIATES AND JOINT                                       於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
   VENTURES
   An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term              聯營公司為本集團擁有一般不少於 20% 股本
   interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting rights       投票權的長期權益的實體，並可對其發揮重
   and over which it is in a position to exercise significant influence.     大影響力。重大影響力指參與投資對象的財
   Significant influence is the power to participate in the financial        政及經營決策之權力，而非控制或共同控制
   and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is not control        該等決策之權力。
   or joint control over those policies.

   A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the parties        合營企業為一項合營安排，據此，擁有安排
   that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net         之共同控制權的人士均有權享有合營企業的
   assets of the joint venture. Joint control is the contractually           淨資產。共同控制乃以合約協定分佔一項安
   agreed sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only            排之控制權，其僅在相關活動決策必須獲分
   when decisions about the relevant activities require unanimous            佔控制權的人士一致同意時方存在。
   consent of the parties sharing control.

   The Group’s investments in associates and joint ventures are              本集團於聯營公司及合營企業的投資乃按權
   stated in the consolidated statement of financial position at the         益會計法核算，按本集團應佔淨資產扣除任
   Group’s share of net assets under the equity method of                    何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況表中呈列。
   accounting, less any impairment losses.

   The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other               本集團應佔聯營公司及合營企業收購後業績
   comprehensive income of associates and joint ventures is                  和其他全面收益分別計入損益及其他全面收
   included in profit or loss and other comprehensive income,                益內。此外，倘直接於聯營公司或合營企業
   respectively. In addition, when there has been a change                   的權益確認變動，則本集團會於綜合權益變
   recognised directly in the equity of the associates or joint              動表確認其應佔的任何變動（如適用）。本集
   ventures, the Group recognises its share of any changes, when             團與其聯營公司或合營企業進行交易而出現
   applicable, in the consolidated statement of changes in equity.           的未實現利益及虧損會按本集團於聯營公司
   Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions between           或合營企 業的權益互相抵銷，但如果未實現
   the Group and its associates or joint ventures are eliminated to          虧損證明所轉讓資產發生減值則除外。收購
   the extent of the Group’s interests in the associates or joint            聯營公司或合營企業產生的商譽計入本集團
   ventures, except where unrealised losses provide evidence of an           於聯營公司或合營企業的投資的一部分。
   impairment of the asset transferred. Goodwill arising from the

   Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition             業務合併乃以購買法入賬。轉讓之代價乃以
   method. The consideration transferred is measured at the                  收購日期的公允價值計算，該公允價值為本
   acquisition-date fair value which is the sum of the acquisition-          集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公允價值、本
   date fair values of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities          集團自被收購方之前度擁有人承擔的負債，
   assumed by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and             及本集團發行以換取被收購方控制權的股本
   the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for control          權益的總和。於各業務合併中，本集團選擇
   of the acquiree. For each business combination, the Group elects          是否以公允價值或被收購方可識別資產淨值
   whether to measure the non-controlling interests in the acquiree          的應佔比例，計算屬現時擁有權權益並賦予
   that are present ownership interests and entitle their holders to a       其持有人有權於清盤時按比例分佔實體的淨
   proportionate share of net assets in the event of liquidation             資產之被收購方的非控制權益。非控制權益
   either at fair value or at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s      的一切其他成分乃按公允價值計量。收購相
   identifiable net assets. All other components of non-controlling          關成本於產生時列為開支。
   interests are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are
   expensed as incurred.

   When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial             當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合約條
   assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and         款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條件，評
   designation in accordance with the contractual terms, economic            估將承接的金融資產及負債，以作出適當的
   circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date.        分類及標示，其中包括分離被收購方主合約
   This includes the separation of embedded derivatives in host              中的嵌入式衍生工具。
   contracts of the acquiree.

   If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously         倘業務合併分階段進行，以往持有的股本權
   held equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition-date fair value     益會以收購日期的公允價值重新計量，任何
   and any resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.           因此帶來的利益或虧損於損益中確認。

   value with changes in fair value recognised in profit or loss.            於損益確認。分類為權益之或然代價並不會
   Contingent consideration that is classified as equity is not              重新計量，其後結算於權益中入賬。
   remeasured and subsequent settlement is accounted for within

   FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT                                                    公允價值之計量
   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or        公允價值為市場參與者於計量日期在有序交
   paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between            易中出售資產所收取的價格或轉讓負債所支
   market participants at the measurement date. The fair value               付的價格。公允價值計量乃假設出售資產或
   measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction              轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要市場或（在
   to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the     無主要市場情況下）最具優勢市場進行而作
   principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a       出。主要或最具優勢市場須為本集團可進入
   principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset           之市場。資產或負債的公允價值乃基於市場
   or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market must          參與者於資產或負債定價所用的假設計量（即
   be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability     假設市場參與者會以最佳經濟利益行事）。
   is measured using the assumptions that market participants
   would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that
   market participants act in their economic best interest.

   A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into              非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及市場參與
   account a market participant’s ability to generate economic               者透過最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用途，
   benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by             或將該資產出售予將最大限度使用該資產達
   selling it to another market participant that would use the asset in      致最佳用途的其他市場參與者以產生經濟利
   its highest and best use.                                                 益的能力。

   The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the           本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備充分數據
   circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to              以供計量公允價值的估值方法，以盡量使用
   measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable             相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少使用不可觀
   inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                     察輸入數據。

   All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or            所有於本綜合財務報表計量或披露公允價值
   disclosed in the consolidated financial statements are                    的資產及負債乃基於對公允價值計量整體而
   categorised within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows,        言屬重大的最低層輸入數據按以下公允價值
   based on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value     層級分類：
   measurement as a whole:

   Level 1   –     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active             第一級   —   基於相同資產或負債於活躍
                   markets for identical assets or liabilities                         市場的報價（未經調整）

   Level 2   –     based on valuation techniques for which the               第二級   —   按估值方法計量，而對公允
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair                  價值計量而言屬重大的最低
                   value measurement is observable, either directly                    層輸入數據可直接或間接觀
                   or indirectly                                                       察得出

   Level 3   –     based on valuation techniques for which the               第三級   —   按估值方法計量，而對公允
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair                  價值計量而言屬重大的最低
                   value measurement is unobservable                                   層輸入數據不可觀察得出

      FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT (Continued)                                       公允價值之計量（續）
      For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the consolidated       就按經常性基準於本綜合財務報表確認的資
      financial statements on a recurring basis, the Group determines          產及負債而言，本集團透過於各報告期末重
      whether transfers have occurred between levels in the hierarchy          新評估分類（基於對公允價值計量整體而言
      by reassessing categorisation (based on the lowest level input           屬重大的最低層輸入數據）確定是否發生不
      that is significant to the fair value measurement as a whole) at the     同層級轉移。
      end of each reporting period.

      IMPAIRMENT OF NON-FINANCIAL ASSETS                                       非金融資產減值
      Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual                倘資產存在減值跡象或須進行年度減值測試
      impairment testing for an asset is required (other than                 （存貨、合約資產、遞延稅項資產及金融資
      inventories, contract asset, deferred tax assets and financial          產除外），則會就該項資產的可收回金額作
      assets), the asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s        出估計。資產的可收回金額為資產或現金產
      recoverable amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-                生單位的可使用價值與其公允價值減出售成
      generating unit’s value in use and its fair value less costs of         本兩者中之較高者，並就個別資產而釐定，
      disposal, and is determined for an individual asset, unless the         除非該項資產並不產生在頗大程度上獨立於
      asset does not generate cash inflows that are largely independent       其他資產或資產組別的現金流入，在此情況
      of those from other assets or groups of assets, in which case the       下，可收回金額以該項資產所屬的現金產生
      recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating unit           單位釐定。
      to which the asset belongs.

      An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount of          資產的賬面值超逾其可收回金額時則確認減
      an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value in           值虧損。於評估可使用價值時，估計未來現
      use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their             金流量將以除稅前貼現率折讓至現值，該貼
      present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current        現率反映現時市場對貨幣時間值及該項資產
      market assessments of the time value of money and the risks              的特定風險的評估。減值虧損將於其產生期
      specific to the asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or        間於損益內與減值資產的功能一致的開支類
      loss in the period in which it arises in those expense categories        別中扣除。
      consistent with the function of the impaired asset.

      An assessment is made at the end of each reporting period as to          於各報告期末會評估是否有跡象顯示於過往
      whether there is an indication that previously recognised                確認的減值虧損可能不再存在或可能已減

      such an indication exists, the recoverable amount is estimated. A        額作出估計。僅於用以釐定資產的可收回金
      previously recognised impairment loss of an asset other than             額的估計出現變動時，過往確認商譽以外的
      goodwill is reversed only if there has been a change in the              資產的減值虧損方可撥回，但撥回金額不得
      estimates used to determine the recoverable amount of that               高於假設過往年度並無就該資產確認減值虧
      asset, but not to an amount higher than the carrying amount that         損而應有之賬面值（扣除任何折舊╱攤銷）。

      amortisation) had no impairment loss been recognised for the
      asset in prior years. A reversal of such an impairment loss is
      credited to profit or loss in the period in which it arises.

      the carrying amount of the asset as a replacement. Where                   換，本集團會確認該等部分為有特定使用年

      be replaced at intervals, the Group recognises such parts as
      individual assets with specific useful lives and depreciates them

      the cost of each item of property, plant and equipment to its              備項目的估計使用年期撇銷其成本至其剩餘

   over their estimated useful lives.

   rewards of ownership of the asset to another entity. If the Group         團方會終止確認金融資產。倘本集團並未轉
   neither transfers nor retains substantially all the risks and rewards     移亦無保留擁有權之絕大部分風險及回報，
   of ownership and continues to control the transferred asset, the          並繼續控制已轉移資產，則本集團繼續按持
   Group continues to recognise the asset to the extent of its               續參與之幅度將資產確認入賬並確認相關負
   continuing involvement and recognises an associated liability. If         債。倘本集團仍保留已轉移金融資產擁有權
   the Group retains substantially all the risks and rewards of              之絕大部分風險及回報，本集團將繼續確認
   ownership of a transferred financial asset, the Group continues to        該金融資產以及將已收所得款項確認為有抵
   recognise the financial asset and also recognises a collateralised        押借貸。
   borrowing for the proceeds received.

   On derecognition of a financial asset in its entirety, the difference     於終止確認全部金融資產時，該項資產賬面
   between the asset’s carrying amount and the sum of the                    值與已收及應收代價及於其他全面收益確認
   consideration received and receivable and the cumulative gain or          並於權益累計之盈虧總和間之差額會於損益
   loss that had been recognised in other comprehensive income               確認。
   and accumulated in equity is recognised in profit or loss.

      IMPAIRMENT OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                              金融資產減值
      The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                本集團就並非按公允價值計入損益的所有債
      (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at FVTPL. ECLs are               務工具確認預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）
      based on the difference between the contractual cash flows due              撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合約應付的
      in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that the             合約現金流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金
      Group expects to receive, discounted at an approximation of the             流量之間的差額，並按接近原有實際利率貼
      original effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will              現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押品或
      include cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit         合約條款所包含的其他信貸增強措施所得的
      enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.                    現金流量。

      General approach                                                            一般方法
      ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which           預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初始確
      there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial      認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸風險而
      recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from           言，會為未來 12 個月內的可能違約事件所產
      default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a               生的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧損撥備（12 個
      12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has               月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始確認以來信貸
      been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a     風險大幅增加的信貸風險而言，不論違約時
      loss allowance is required for credit losses expected over the              間如何，須就預期於風險餘下存續期內的信
      remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the           貸虧損計提虧損撥備（整個存續期預期信貸
      default (a lifetime ECL).                                                   虧損）。

   At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit             於各報告日期，本集團評估自初始確認後金
   risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since          融工具的信貸風險是否大幅增加。於進行評
   initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group                估時，本集團將於報告日期金融工具發生違
   compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial                 約的風險與初始確認日期金融工具發生違約
   instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default            的風險進行比較，並考慮所有毋須付出不必
   occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial           要成本或努力即可獲得的合理支持資料，包
   recognition and considers reasonable and supportable                      括歷史及前瞻性資料。
   information that is available without undue cost or effort,
   including historical and forward-looking information.

   The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual         當合約付款已逾期 5 年，本集團認為金融資
   payments are 5 years past due. However, in certain cases, the             產將會違約。可是，於某些情況下，倘內部
   Group may also consider a financial asset to be in default when           或外部資料顯示在並無計及本集團任何信貸
   internal or external information indicates that the Group is              增強措施前，本集團不大可能悉數收到未償
   unlikely to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in full           還合約款項，本集團亦可能認為金融資產將
   before taking into account any credit enhancements held by the            會違約。於無法合理預期收回合約現金流量
   Group. A financial asset is written off when there is no reasonable       時撇銷金融資產。
   expectation of recovering the contractual cash flows.

   Manufacturing and trading                                                 製造和貿易
   Cost is determined on the weighted average basis and, in the              成本乃以加權平均法釐定，而就在產品及產

   disposal.

      PROVISIONS                                                             撥備
      A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or          倘因過往事件導致現有債務（法定或推定）及
      constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is         日後可能需要有資源流出以償還債務，則確
      probable that a future outflow of resources will be required to        認撥備，但必須能可靠估計有關債務金額。
      settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be
      made of the amount of the obligation.

      When the effect of discounting is material, the amount                 倘折現的影響重大，則就撥備確認的金額為
      recognised for a provision is the present value at the end of the      預期需用作償還債務的未來支出於報告期末
      reporting period of the future expenditures expected to be             的現值。因時間流逝而產生的折現現值增額
      required to settle the obligation. The increase in the discounted      會計入損益內的融資成本。
      present value amount arising from the passage of time is
      included in finance costs in profit or loss.

      The Group provides for warranties in relation to the sale of           於保修期間，本集團就銷售若干工業產品及
      certain industrial products and the provision of construction          提供建築服務發生的缺陷提供一般維修的擔
      services for general repairs of defects occurring during the           保。本集團提供該等保證型擔保乃根據銷量
      warranty period. Provisions for these assurance-type warranties        以及過往維修及退貨程度經驗確認，並貼現
      granted by the Group are recognised based on sales volume and          至其現值（如適當）。
      past experience of the level of repairs and returns, discounted to
      their present values as appropriate.

      INCOME TAX                                                             所得稅
      Income tax comprises current and deferred tax. Income tax              所得稅包括即期及遞延稅項。與並非於損益
      relating to items recognised outside profit or loss is recognised      賬確認的項目有關的所得稅會在並非損益賬
      outside profit or loss, either in other comprehensive income or        的其他全面收益內或直接在權益內確認。
      directly in equity.

      Current tax assets and liabilities are measured at the amount          即期稅項資產及負債按預期獲稅務機構退回
      expected to be recovered from or paid to the taxation                  或向稅務機構支付的款項計量。計量的基準
      authorities, based on tax rates (and tax laws) that have been          為報告期末已實行或已實際執行的稅率（及
      enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting           稅法），亦考慮本集團經營所在國家現行的
      period, taking into consideration interpretations and practices        詮釋及慣例。
      prevailing in the countries in which the Group operates.

      Deferred tax is provided, using the liability method, on all           遞延稅項乃採用負債法就報告期末資產及負
      temporary differences at the end of the reporting period               債的稅基與其用作財務呈報的賬面值之間的
      between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying     所有暫時差額作出撥備。
      amounts for financial reporting purposes.

   significant revenue reversal in the amount of cumulative revenue        不會發生收入大幅撥回。
   recognised will not occur when the associated uncertainty with
   the variable consideration is subsequently resolved.

   provides the customer with a significant benefit of financing the       服務而為客戶提供重大融資利益超過一年

   reflected in a separate financing transaction between the Group         集團提供超過一年重大融資利益的融資部分

   contains a financing component which provides the Group a               法計算的合約負債所附加的利息開支。對於

     Fair value of investment properties                                            投資物業公允價值
     The best evidence of fair value is current prices in an active                 公允價值之最佳憑證為相同地點及狀況之物
     market for properties in the same location and condition and                   業當時於活躍市場之價格，並受類似租約及
     subject to similar leases and other contracts. In the absence of               其他合約規限。在欠缺該等資料之情況下，
     such information, management determines the amount within a                    管理層根據合理公允價值估計範圍釐定有關
     range of reasonable fair value estimates. In making its judgement,             數額。在作出有關估算時，管理層會考慮不
     management considers information from a variety of sources,                    同 來 源 之 資 料， 包 括：(i) 獨 立 估 值；(ii) 性
     including (i) independent valuations; (ii) current prices in an active         質、狀況或地點不同（或受不同租約或其他
     market for properties of a different nature, condition and location            合約規限）之物業當時於活躍市場之價格，
     (or subject to different leases or other contracts), adjusted to               並加以調整以反映有關差異；(iii) 類似物業
     reflect those differences; (iii) recent prices of similar properties in        於較不活躍市場之近期價格       （或會作出調整，
     less active markets, with adjustments to reflect any changes in                以反映自按交易之日以來經濟狀況之任何轉
     economic conditions since the dates of transactions that occurred              變對價格之影響）；及 (iv) 根據對未來現金流
     at those prices; and (iv) discounted cash flow projections, based              量之可靠估計而預測之貼現現金流量，輔以
     on reliable estimates of future cash flows, derived from the terms             任何現有租約與其他合約之條款及（如可能）
     of any existing lease and other contracts, and (where possible)                外在憑證（如地點及狀況相同之類似物業之
     from external evidence such as current market rates for similar                當時市場租金），並採用反映當時市場對現
     properties in the same location and condition, and using discount              金流量金額及時間之不確定性評估之貼現率
     rates that reflect current market assessments of the uncertainty in            計算。
     the amount and timing of cash flows.

     Leases — Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                             租賃 — 估算增量借款利率
     The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a             本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利率，
     lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”)            因此使用增量借款利率（「增量借款利率」）計
     to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the         量租賃負債。增量借款利率為本集團於類似
     Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with                經濟環境中為取得與使用權資產價值相近之
     a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a                資產，而以類似借期與類似抵押品借入所需
     similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic                  資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款利率反
     environment. The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would                  映了本集團「應支付」的款項，當無可觀察
     have to pay”, which requires estimation when no observable                     的利率時（如就並無訂立融資交易之附屬公
     rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into           司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以反映租賃
     financing transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to reflect             之條款及條件時（如當租賃並非以附屬公司
     the terms and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases                之功能貨幣訂立時），則須對利率作出估計。
     are not in the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group                    當可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）可用時，本
     estimates the IBR using observable inputs (such as market                      集團用此估算增量借款利率，並須作出若干
     interest rates) when available and is required to make certain                 實體特定的估計（如附屬公司的獨立信貸評
     entity-specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s stand-alone                級）。
     credit rating).

      Performance obligations                                                    履約責任
      Information about the Group’s performance obligations is                   有關本集團履約責任的資料概述如下：
      summarised below:

      Sale of goods                                                              銷售貨品
      The performance obligation is satisfied upon delivery of the               本集團於交貨時履行責任，付款一般在交貨
      goods and payment is generally due within 30 to 360 days from              後 30 至 360 天內到期，惟新客戶一般須預先
      delivery, except for new customers, where payment in advance is            付款。
      normally required.

      Renovation, installation and engineering services                          裝修、安裝及工程服務
      The performance obligation is satisfied over time as services are          履約責任隨提供服務而隨時間推移履行，且
      rendered and payment is generally due within 30 days from the              付款一般於結算日起 30 天內到期。客戶保留
      date of billing. A certain percentage of payment is retained by            一定百分比的付款直至保留期結束，乃由於
      customers until the end of the retention period as the Group’s             本集團獲取最終付款的權利視乎於客戶按合
      entitlement to the final payment is conditional on the satisfaction        約規定的一段時間內對服務質素表示滿意後
      of the service quality by the customers over a certain period as           方可作實。
      stipulated in the contracts.

13. LEASES                                                                  13. 租賃
      THE GROUP AS A LESSEE                                                   本集團作為承租人
      The Group has lease contracts for various items of leasehold            本集團擁有多個用於營運的租賃業權土地、
      land, plants and buildings, motor vehicles and other equipment
                                                                              廠房及樓宇、汽車及其他設備的租賃合約。
      used in its operations. For leasehold land classified as prepaid
                                                                              對於先前分類為預付土地租賃款的租賃業權
      land lease payments previously, lump sum payments were made
                                                                              土地，向擁有人收購租賃期為 50 年的土地
      upfront to acquire the leasehold land from the owners with lease
      periods of 50 years, and no ongoing payments will be made               時，已預先作出一次性付款，且根據該等土
      under the terms of these land leases. For other leased lands            地租賃的條款，將不會進行後續付款。對於
      classified as operating lease previously, they generally have lease     先前分類為經營租賃的其他租賃土地，其租
      terms between 20 and 50 years. Lease of plants and buildings            期通常介乎 20 年至 50 年。廠房及樓宇租賃
      generally have lease terms between 1 and 17 years, while motor          的租期通常介乎 1 年至 17 年，而汽車及其他
      vehicles and other equipment generally have lease terms                 設備的租期通常介乎1年至3年。一般而言，
      between 1 and 3 years. Generally, the Group is restricted from          本集團被限制不能於本集團以外轉讓及轉租

   CREDIT RISK                                                             信貸風險
   Credit risk is the risk that a counterparty will not meet its           信貸風險指因交易對手無法履行其於金融工
   obligations under a financial instrument or customer contract,          具或客戶合約項下責任而蒙受財務虧損的風
   leading to a financial loss. The Group is exposed to credit risk in     險。本集團所面臨的信貸風險與其貿易應收
   relation to its trade and bills receivables, contract assets, loan      款項及票據、合約資產、應收借款、其他應
   receivables, other receivables, debt instruments at FVTOCI and          收款項、按公允價值計入其他全面收益的債
   cash and bank deposits.                                                 務工具以及現金及銀行存款有關。

   The concession operating right of the Group represent sewage            本集團的特許經營權指有關建造、營運及移
   treatment service concession financial receivables in respect of        交（「建造、營運及移交」）安排的特許污水處
   Build-operate-transfer (“BOT”) arrangements. Since the parties          理服務的金融應收款項。於報告期末，鑑於
   to BOT arrangements are local government authorities in the             建造、營運及移交安排的訂約方均為中國地
   PRC, the Group considers the credit risk is low at the end of the       方政府機關，故本集團認為信貸風險偏低。
   reporting period. The Group does not hold any collateral over           本集團並無就有關結餘持有任何抵押品。
   these balances.

   In relation to the Group’s financial services business, the Group       有關本集團的金融服務業務，本集團擁有信
   has a credit team which is responsible for the evaluation of            貸團隊負責評估客戶的信貸評級、財務背景
   customers’ credit ratings, financial background and repayment           及償還能力。本集團僅向信譽良好的客戶提
   abilities. The Group only provides credit to customers with             供信貸及一般須提供抵押品。該等客戶的付
   creditworthiness and collateral is normally required. The risk of       款拖欠風險並不重大。
   default in payment by these customers is considered not
   significant.

   The Group has policies in place to ensure that sales are made to        本集團訂有政策以確保向擁有穩健財政實力
   purchasers with an appropriate financial strength and appropriate       及可支付適當百分比之首期付款的買方作出
   percentage of down payments. It also has other monitoring               銷售，亦設有其他監控程序以確保採取跟進
   procedures to ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover          行動收回逾期債務。此外，本集團定期審閱
   overdue debts. In addition, the Group reviews regularly the             各項個別貿易應收款項及票據和合約資產的
   recoverable amount of each individual trade and bills receivables       可收回金額，以確保就不可收回金額作出足
   and contract assets to ensure that adequate impairment losses           夠的減值虧損。本集團的信貸風險源自多名
   are made for irrecoverable amounts. The Group has no significant        交易對手及客戶，故並無重大信貸集中風險。
   concentrations of credit risk, with exposure spread over a large
   number of counterparties and customers.

   For other receivables, an impairment analysis is performed by           就其他應收款項而言，管理層透過考慮歷史
   management at the end of each reporting period by considering           結算記錄、過往經驗及將來經濟狀況於各報
   historical settlement records, past experience and future               告期末進行減值分析。本公司董事認為，本
   economic conditions. The directors of the Company believe that          集團其他應收款項的未償還結餘並無重大固
   there is no material credit risk inherent in the Group’s                有信貸風險。
   outstanding balance of other receivables.

   CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                      資本管理
   The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to         本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本集團繼
   safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as going concern and          續以持續基準經營的能力，以及維持合理的
   to maintain reasonable capital ratios in order to support its           資本比率支持其業務，以及將股東的價值提
   business and maximise shareholders’ value.                              升至最高。


   The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments           本集團根據經濟狀況變動管理其資本架構及
   to it in light of changes in economic conditions. In order to           作出調整。為優化債務及權益結餘，本集團
   optimise the debt and equity balance, the Group may adjust the          可調整支付予股東的股息、向股東退回資
   dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders,       本、發 行 新 股 或 新 債 或 贖 回 現 有 債 務。年
   issue new shares or new debts or the redemption of existing             內，管理資本的目標、政策或過程並無變動。
   debts. No changes were made in the objectives, policies or
   processes for managing capital during the year.

   The Group monitors capital using a net debt to equity ratio,            本集團以債務淨額對權益比率監控資本，該
   which is net debt divided by capital. Net debt includes                 比率為債務淨額除以資本。債務淨額包括借
   borrowings, convertible loans and lease liabilities less cash and       款、可換股貸款及租賃負債減現金及銀行存
   bank deposits. Capital represents the total equity.                     款。資本乃指權益總額。


   The Group’s strategy is to maintain the net debt to equity ratio at     本集團的策略為維持債務淨額對權益比率在
   a healthy level in order to support its businesses. The principal       健康水平，以支持其業務。本集團採取的主
   strategies adopted by the Group include, without limitation,            要策略包括但不限於審閱未來現金流量需求
   reviewing future cash flow requirements and the ability to meet         及支付到期債務的能力，保持可用銀行融資
   debt repayment schedules when they fall due, maintaining a              在合理水平及調整投資計劃及融資計劃（如
   reasonable level of available banking facilities and adjusting          需要），以確保本集團擁有合理水平的資本
   investment plans and financing plans, if necessary, to ensure that      支持其業務。於報告期末的債務淨額對權益
   the Group has a reasonable level of capital to support its              比率如下：
   business. The net debt to equity ratios, at the end of the
